```c
/* ---- the bank code (M4.6c1 Task 4) ---- */

static void check_row(const char *name, uint16_t addr, bool ramcode)
{
    const fw_replacement *r = fw_replacement_find(name);
    CHECK(r != NULL);
    if (!r)
        return;
    CHECK_EQ(r->addr, addr);
    CHECK_EQ(r->outputs, FW_OUT_A | FW_OUT_X | FW_OUT_Y);
    CHECK(!r->waits && !r->isr);
    CHECK_EQ(r->ramcode, ramcode);
}

/* The nine rows: the RAM-code routines hooked with machine_hook_ramcode, bank_load_ramcode in the main ROM; A, X and
   Y declared (the thunk comments name what the callers read). */
static void test_rows(void)
{
    check_row("bank_load_ramcode", ROM_BANK_LOAD_RAMCODE, false);
    check_row("bank_enter", BANK_ENTER, true);
    check_row("bank_scan", BANK_SCAN, true);
    check_row("bank_select", BANK_SELECT, true);
    check_row("bank_probe", BANK_PROBE, true);
    check_row("bank_read_id", BANK_READ_ID, true);
    check_row("bank_put_id_char", BANK_PUT_ID_CHAR, true);
    check_row("bank_safe_char", BANK_SAFE_CHAR, true);
    check_row("bank_find_current", BANK_FIND_CURRENT, true);
}

/* RAM after setup, then the RAM copy as bank_load_ramcode makes it and the version copies boot_init_settings makes
   ($ADF1-$ADFA: bank_ver_major = $FFE0, bank_ver_minor = $FFE1), P2 = the main bank, nothing logged. */
static void setup_banks(void)
{
    setup();
    for (uint16_t i = 0; i <= BANK_RAMCODE_LAST - BANK_RAMCODE; i++)
        g_ram[BANK_RAMCODE + i] = fw_rom8(&g_f, (uint16_t)(BANK_RAMCODE_IMAGE + i));
    g_ram[BANK_VER_MAJOR] = fw_rom8(&g_f, BANK_HDR_VERSION);
    g_ram[BANK_VER_MINOR] = fw_rom8(&g_f, BANK_HDR_VERSION + 1);
    g_sfr[SFR_P2] = P2_MAIN_BANK;
}

/* bank_load_ramcode $A600: Y, X and P2 (one read) saved in $78, $76, $75; $ADFE-$AFFC copied to $0200-$03FE and
   nothing beyond; A and Y kept, X = 0. */
static void test_load_ramcode(void)
{
    static const event want[] = { { EV_READ, SFR_P2, 0xF1, 0, 0 } };
    setup();
    g_sfr[SFR_P2] = 0xF1;                         /* the window on, the main bank, P2.0 set */
    g_ram[BANK_RAMCODE - 1] = 0xA5;
    g_ram[BANK_RAMCODE_LAST + 1] = 0xA5;
    fw_regs r = { 0x05, 0x12, 0x34, 0 };
    bank_load_ramcode(&g_f, &r);
    check_log(want, 1);
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[FW_TMP_78], 0x34);
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[FW_TMP_76], 0x12);
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[FW_TMP_75], 0xF1);
    unsigned differ = 0;
    for (uint16_t i = 0; i <= BANK_RAMCODE_LAST - BANK_RAMCODE; i++)
        differ += g_ram[BANK_RAMCODE + i] != fw_rom8(&g_f, (uint16_t)(BANK_RAMCODE_IMAGE + i));
    CHECK_EQ(differ, 0);
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[BANK_P2_TABLE + 5], P2_BANK_5);
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[BANK_ENTER], 0x48);            /* bank_enter's PHA */
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[BANK_RAMCODE - 1], 0xA5);
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[BANK_RAMCODE_LAST + 1], 0xA5);
    CHECK_EQ(r.a, 0x05);
    CHECK_EQ(r.x, 0);
    CHECK_EQ(r.y, 0x34);
    CHECK(!g_f.fault);
}

/* bank_select $025E: X = the table entry ($0200,X; with boot_dip bit 1 $0208,X); $77 = P2 & $83; P2 = X | $77 (a read,
   then a write); f->bank follows. A and Y kept. A bank number of 8-15 indexes $0208-$020F (Review Focus 1): 15 reads
   bank_p2_table_alt[7], the main bank. */
static void test_select(void)
{
    static const event want[] = { { EV_READ, SFR_P2, 0xF1, 0, 0 }, { EV_WRITE, SFR_P2, 0xF9, 0, 0 } };
    setup_banks();
    g_sfr[SFR_P2] = 0xF1;
    fw_regs r = { 5, 0x11, 0x22, 0 };
    bank_select(&g_f, &r);
    check_log(want, 2);
    CHECK_EQ(r.x, P2_BANK_5);
    CHECK_EQ(r.a, 5);
    CHECK_EQ(r.y, 0x22);
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[TN_RESUME_POS], 0x81);
    CHECK_EQ(g_f.bank, P2_BANK_5);
    CHECK(g_f.low_window);

    g_ram[BOOT_DIP] = DIP_ALT_BANKS;              /* bank_p2_table_alt[5]: the main bank */
    r.a = 5;
    bank_select(&g_f, &r);
    CHECK_EQ(r.x, P2_MAIN_BANK);
    CHECK_EQ(g_sfr[SFR_P2], 0xF1);
    r.a = 2;
    bank_select(&g_f, &r);
    CHECK_EQ(r.x, P2_BANK_5);

    g_ram[BOOT_DIP] = 0;
    r.a = 15;
    bank_select(&g_f, &r);
    CHECK_EQ(r.x, P2_MAIN_BANK);
    r.a = 10;                                     /* $020A: bank_p2_table_alt[2] */
    bank_select(&g_f, &r);
    CHECK_EQ(r.x, P2_BANK_5);
    r.a = 0;
    bank_select(&g_f, &r);
    CHECK_EQ(r.x, P2_MAIN_BANK);
    CHECK(!g_f.fault);
}

/* bank_probe $0277: bank_absent bit 0 set, then cleared when $FFD0 reads $78: the main bank's image (no host call), or
   another bank through read_bank ($FF: an empty socket, bit 0 stays set). Its other bits kept. */
static void test_probe(void)
{
    static const event want[] = { { EV_BANK, BANK_ENTRY_COLD, 0xFF, 0, 0 }, { EV_BANK, BANK_ENTRY_COLD, 0x78, 0, 0 } };
    setup_banks();
    g_ram[BANK_ABSENT] = 0xF1;
    fw_regs r = { 1, 2, 3, 0 };
    bank_probe(&g_f, &r);
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[BANK_ABSENT], 0xF0);
    CHECK_EQ(g_nlog, 0);
    g_f.bank = P2_BANK_7;
    bank_probe(&g_f, &r);
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[BANK_ABSENT], 0xF1);
    g_other[BANK_ENTRY_COLD] = BANK_SIGNATURE;
    bank_probe(&g_f, &r);
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[BANK_ABSENT], 0xF0);
    check_log(want, 2);
    CHECK_EQ(r.a, 1);
    CHECK_EQ(r.x, 2);
    CHECK_EQ(r.y, 3);
}

/* bank_safe_char $02E9 and bank_put_id_char $02E1: below $20 becomes '*', the rest is kept; the byte stored at
   bank_id_table + X, X + 1 with an 8-bit wrap. */
static void test_put_id_char(void)
{
    setup();
    fw_regs r = { 0x1F, 0, 0x77, 0 };
    bank_safe_char(&g_f, &r);
    CHECK_EQ(r.a, '*');
    r.a = 0x20;
    bank_safe_char(&g_f, &r);
    CHECK_EQ(r.a, 0x20);
    r.a = 0xFF;
    bank_safe_char(&g_f, &r);
    CHECK_EQ(r.a, 0xFF);
    r.a = 0x00;
    r.x = 0xFF;
    bank_put_id_char(&g_f, &r);
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[BANK_ID_TABLE + 0xFF], '*');
    CHECK_EQ(r.a, '*');
    CHECK_EQ(r.x, 0);
    CHECK_EQ(r.y, 0x77);
    CHECK_EQ(g_nlog, 0);
}

/* bank_read_id $0285: bank 1 present (the main bank's image): its ID "000440" "0201" "0404" at bank_id_table + 0,
   X = 14, Y = 6; bank 7 absent: 14 spaces at + 84, X = 98, Y = 14; another bank present through read_bank, its
   control codes as '*'; bank 0 (the add loop runs 255 times: 14 x 255 = 242 in 8 bits) absent: spaces at + 242-255,
   X = 0. A kept. */
static void test_read_id(void)
{
    setup_banks();
    fw_regs r = { 1, 0x55, 0x66, 0 };
    bank_read_id(&g_f, &r);
    CHECK(memcmp(g_ram + BANK_ID_TABLE, "00044002010404", 14) == 0);
    CHECK_EQ(r.a, 1);
    CHECK_EQ(r.x, 14);
    CHECK_EQ(r.y, 6);
    CHECK_EQ(g_nlog, 0);

    g_ram[BANK_ABSENT] = 1;
    r.a = 7;
    bank_read_id(&g_f, &r);
    CHECK(memcmp(g_ram + BANK_ID_TABLE + 84, "              ", 14) == 0);
    CHECK_EQ(r.a, 7);
    CHECK_EQ(r.x, 98);
    CHECK_EQ(r.y, 14);

    g_ram[BANK_ABSENT] = 0;
    g_f.bank = P2_BANK_3;
    memcpy(g_other + BANK_HDR_LANG_CODE, "AB\x01" "CDE", 6);
    memcpy(g_other + BANK_HDR_ENGINE_VER_TEXT, "1234", 4);
    memcpy(g_other + BANK_HDR_LANG_VER_TEXT, "\x1F" "678", 4);
    r.a = 3;
    bank_read_id(&g_f, &r);
    CHECK(memcmp(g_ram + BANK_ID_TABLE + 28, "AB*CDE1234*678", 14) == 0);
    CHECK_EQ(g_nlog, 14);                          /* every byte through read_bank */
    CHECK_EQ(g_log[6].addr, BANK_HDR_ENGINE_VER_TEXT);
    CHECK_EQ(g_log[13].addr, BANK_HDR_LANG_VER_TEXT + 3);

    g_ram[BANK_ABSENT] = 1;
    r.a = 0;
    bank_read_id(&g_f, &r);
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[BANK_ID_TABLE + 242], ' ');
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[BANK_ID_TABLE + 255], ' ');
    CHECK_EQ(r.x, 0);
    CHECK_EQ(r.y, 14);
    CHECK(!g_f.fault);
}

/* bank_find_current $02F0: one read of P2; bank_current = the first bank 1-7 whose entry equals P2 & $7C (bank 1 for
   the main bank, though entry 0 holds it too); bank_p2_table_alt with boot_dip bit 1; unchanged when no entry
   matches. A, X and Y kept. */
static void test_find_current(void)
{
    static const event want[] = { { EV_READ, SFR_P2, 0x7B, 0, 0 } };
    setup_banks();
    g_sfr[SFR_P2] = 0x7B;                         /* bank bits $78, P2.0 and P2.1 set */
    fw_regs r = { 1, 2, 3, 0 };
    bank_find_current(&g_f, &r);
    check_log(want, 1);
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[BANK_CURRENT], 5);
    g_ram[BOOT_DIP] = DIP_ALT_BANKS;
    bank_find_current(&g_f, &r);
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[BANK_CURRENT], 2);
    g_sfr[SFR_P2] = P2_MAIN_BANK;
    bank_find_current(&g_f, &r);
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[BANK_CURRENT], 1);
    g_sfr[SFR_P2] = 0x0C;                         /* in neither table */
    g_ram[BANK_CURRENT] = 9;
    bank_find_current(&g_f, &r);
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[BANK_CURRENT], 9);
    g_ram[BOOT_DIP] = 0;
    bank_find_current(&g_f, &r);
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[BANK_CURRENT], 9);
    CHECK_EQ(r.a, 1);
    CHECK_EQ(r.x, 2);
    CHECK_EQ(r.y, 3);
    CHECK(!g_f.fault);
}

/* bank_scan $0241 with $75/$76/$78 as bank_load_ramcode leaves them: banks 7 down to 2 selected (a P2 read and write
   each) and probed through read_bank (empty sockets: 14 spaces each), bank 1 the main bank (its ID from the image);
   then P2 = $75 written back, X = $76, Y = $78, A kept. */
static void test_scan(void)
{
    setup_banks();
    g_ram[FW_TMP_75] = P2_MAIN_BANK;
    g_ram[FW_TMP_76] = 0x11;
    g_ram[FW_TMP_78] = 0x22;
    fw_regs r = { 0x33, 0, 0, 0 };
    bank_scan(&g_f, &r);
    CHECK_EQ(g_nlog, 6 * 3 + 2 + 1);
    CHECK_EQ(g_log[0].kind, EV_READ);             /* bank 7: select, probe */
    CHECK_EQ(g_log[1].kind, EV_WRITE);
    CHECK_EQ(g_log[1].a, P2_BANK_7);
    CHECK_EQ(g_log[2].kind, EV_BANK);
    CHECK_EQ(g_log[2].addr, BANK_ENTRY_COLD);
    CHECK_EQ(g_log[16].a, P2_BANK_2);             /* bank 2's P2 write */
    CHECK_EQ(g_log[19].a, P2_MAIN_BANK);          /* bank 1's */
    CHECK_EQ(g_log[20].kind, EV_WRITE);           /* bank_restore */
    CHECK_EQ(g_log[20].a, P2_MAIN_BANK);
    CHECK(memcmp(g_ram + BANK_ID_TABLE, "00044002010404", 14) == 0);
    for (unsigned i = 14; i < 98; i++)
        CHECK_EQ(g_ram[BANK_ID_TABLE + i], ' ');
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[BANK_ABSENT] & 1, 0);          /* bank 1, probed last */
    CHECK_EQ(r.a, 0x33);
    CHECK_EQ(r.x, 0x11);
    CHECK_EQ(r.y, 0x22);
    CHECK_EQ(g_f.bank, P2_MAIN_BANK);
    CHECK(!g_f.fault);
}

/* bank_enter $0210, an absent bank (5): select, probe, then bank_restore: P2 = $75, A kept, X = $76, Y = $78; no
   entry. */
static void test_enter_absent(void)
{
    static const event want[] = {
        { EV_READ, SFR_P2, P2_MAIN_BANK, 0, 0 }, { EV_WRITE, SFR_P2, P2_BANK_5, 0, 0 },
        { EV_BANK, BANK_ENTRY_COLD, 0xFF, 0, 0 }, { EV_WRITE, SFR_P2, P2_MAIN_BANK, 0, 0 },
    };
    setup_banks();
    g_ram[FW_TMP_75] = P2_MAIN_BANK;
    g_ram[FW_TMP_76] = 0x44;
    g_ram[FW_TMP_78] = 0x55;
    fw_regs r = { 5, 0, 0, 0 };
    bank_enter(&g_f, &r);
    check_log(want, 4);
    CHECK_EQ(r.a, 5);
    CHECK_EQ(r.x, 0x44);
    CHECK_EQ(r.y, 0x55);
    CHECK(!g_f.unwound);
    CHECK(!g_f.fault);
}

/* bank_enter, the main bank present: warm ($FFD4, A = $FFE0, X = $FF) when $FFE1 and $FFE0 equal bank_ver_minor and
   bank_ver_major, zero page untouched; else cold ($FFD0, A = 0, X = 0) with $01-$64 cleared ($00 and $65 kept): the
   minor differing (the major not read), or the major alone. Bank 0 is the main bank too (Review Focus 1). Another
   present bank's header is read through read_bank, $FFE1 then $FFE0. */
static void test_enter_present(void)
{
    static const event warm[] = {
        { EV_READ, SFR_P2, P2_MAIN_BANK, 0, 0 }, { EV_WRITE, SFR_P2, P2_MAIN_BANK, 0, 0 },
        { EV_ENTER, BANK_ENTRY_WARM, 2, 0xFF, 0 },
    };
    static const event cold[] = {
        { EV_READ, SFR_P2, P2_MAIN_BANK, 0, 0 }, { EV_WRITE, SFR_P2, P2_MAIN_BANK, 0, 0 },
        { EV_ENTER, BANK_ENTRY_COLD, 0, 0, 0 },
    };
    static const event other[] = {
        { EV_READ, SFR_P2, P2_MAIN_BANK, 0, 0 }, { EV_WRITE, SFR_P2, P2_BANK_6, 0, 0 },
        { EV_BANK, BANK_ENTRY_COLD, BANK_SIGNATURE, 0, 0 }, { EV_BANK, BANK_HDR_VERSION + 1, 1, 0, 0 },
        { EV_BANK, BANK_HDR_VERSION, 2, 0, 0 }, { EV_ENTER, BANK_ENTRY_WARM, 2, 0xFF, 0 },
    };
    for (uint8_t bank = 0; bank <= 1; bank++) {
        setup_banks();
        memset(g_ram, 0x5A, 0x66);
        g_ram[BOOT_DIP] = 0;
        fw_regs r = { bank, 0, 0, 0 };
        bank_enter(&g_f, &r);
        check_log(warm, 3);
        CHECK(g_f.unwound);
        CHECK_EQ(g_ram[0x01], 0x5A);
        CHECK_EQ(g_ram[0x64], 0x5A);
    }

    setup_banks();
    memset(g_ram, 0x5A, 0x66);
    g_ram[BOOT_DIP] = 0;
    g_ram[BANK_VER_MINOR]++;
    fw_regs r = { 1, 0, 0, 0 };
    bank_enter(&g_f, &r);
    check_log(cold, 3);
    CHECK(g_f.unwound);
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[0x00], 0x5A);
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[0x01], 0);
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[0x64], 0);
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[0x65], 0x5A);

    setup_banks();
    g_ram[BANK_VER_MAJOR]++;
    bank_enter(&g_f, &r);
    check_log(cold, 3);

    setup_banks();
    g_other[BANK_ENTRY_COLD] = BANK_SIGNATURE;
    g_other[BANK_HDR_VERSION] = 2;
    g_other[BANK_HDR_VERSION + 1] = 1;
    r.a = 6;
    bank_enter(&g_f, &r);
    check_log(other, 6);
    CHECK(g_f.unwound);
    CHECK(!g_f.fault);
}

void suite_fw_bank(void)
{
    CHECK(fw_image_get() != NULL);
```

```c
/* The modes in which the C reaches cmd_run_due's dispatch, so @= entering a bank resets the stack under C and the
   C call chain unwinds (M4.2 spec 5.3). */
static bool runs_c_dispatch(const char *only)
{
```

**Create `src/firmware/bank.h`:**

```c
#ifndef FW_BANK_H
#define FW_BANK_H
/* The bank code (ROM $A600 and the RAM copy $0210-$0312), decompiled (M4.6c1 spec 2.1). The RAM-code routines read
   the bank tables from the RAM copy ($0200,X and $0208,X), every byte of the selected bank through fw_rom8 (so under
   another bank through the host's read_bank) and P2 through the SFR helpers, in the ROM's order. Each takes A, X and
   Y in r and leaves them as the ROM does; the PHP / SEI ... PLP pairs are invisible to the C (nothing else runs while
   it runs), and no caller reads a flag. */
#include "fw.h"

/* $A600: $78 = Y, $76 = X, $75 = P2 (one read); bank_ramcode_image $ADFE-$AFFC copied to $0200-$03FE in 256 passes
   of $ADFE,X -> $0200,X and $AEFD,X -> $02FF,X ($02FF is written twice, with the same byte). A and Y kept, X = 0. */
void bank_load_ramcode(fw *f, fw_regs *r);
/* $0210: A = the bank. bank_select, bank_probe; an absent bank: bank_restore (P2 = $75, X = $76, Y = $78, A kept).
   Else the bank entry through fw_enter: $FFD4 (warm) with A = the bank's $FFE0 and X = $FF when its $FFE1 and $FFE0
   equal bank_ver_minor and bank_ver_major (read in that order, $FFE0 only when $FFE1 is equal); else $01-$64 cleared
   and $FFD0 (cold) with A = 0 and X = 0. fw_enter does not return while a trap is set. */
void bank_enter(fw *f, fw_regs *r);
/* $0241: for A = 7 down to 1: bank_select, bank_probe, bank_read_id; then bank_restore: P2 = $75 (one write), X = $76,
   Y = $78. A kept. */
void bank_scan(fw *f, fw_regs *r);
/* $025E: A = the bank: X = bank_p2_table[A] ($0200,X, an 8-bit index: 8-15 read bank_p2_table_alt), or
   bank_p2_table_alt[A] ($0208,X) while boot_dip bit 1 is set; $77 = P2 & $83 (one read); P2 = X | $77 (one write).
   A and Y kept. */
void bank_select(fw *f, fw_regs *r);
/* $0277: bank_absent bit 0 set, then cleared when the selected bank reads BANK_SIGNATURE ($78) at $FFD0. A, X and Y
   kept. */
void bank_probe(fw *f, fw_regs *r);
/* $0285: A = the bank. X = 14 x (A - 1) by an 8-bit add loop (A = 0 runs it 255 times); while bank_absent bit 0 is
   set 14 spaces to bank_id_table + X, Y = 14; else bank_put_id_char of the 6 bytes at $FFE2,Y, then $9800-$9803 and
   $FFEA-$FFED, Y = 6. A kept; X after the last byte written (8 bits). */
void bank_read_id(fw *f, fw_regs *r);
/* $02E1: bank_safe_char, then bank_id_table[X] = A, X + 1 (8 bits). Y kept. */
void bank_put_id_char(fw *f, fw_regs *r);
/* $02E9: A below $20 becomes '*'. X and Y kept. */
void bank_safe_char(fw *f, fw_regs *r);
/* $02F0: P2 & $7C (one read of P2); bank_current = the first X in 1-7 whose table entry equals it (the table chosen by
   boot_dip bit 1 at each pass); unchanged when none does. A, X and Y kept. */
void bank_find_current(fw *f, fw_regs *r);

#endif
```

**Create `src/firmware/bank.c`:**

```c
#include "bank.h"
#include "bank_addr.h"

#define SFR_P2  0xF0

void bank_load_ramcode(fw *f, fw_regs *r)
{
    fw_wr8(f, FW_TMP_78, r->y);                                    /* STY $78 */
    fw_wr8(f, FW_TMP_76, r->x);                                    /* STX $76 */
    fw_wr8(f, FW_TMP_75, fw_sfr8(f, SFR_P2));                      /* LDX P2 / STX $75 */
    uint8_t x = 0;
    do {                                                           /* LDA $ADFE,X / STA $0200,X / LDA $AEFD,X /
                                                                      STA $02FF,X / INX / BNE */
        fw_wr8(f, (uint16_t)(BANK_RAMCODE + x), fw_rom8(f, (uint16_t)(BANK_RAMCODE_IMAGE + x)));
        fw_wr8(f, (uint16_t)(BANK_RAMCODE + 0xFF + x), fw_rom8(f, (uint16_t)(BANK_RAMCODE_IMAGE + 0xFF + x)));
    } while (++x != 0);
    r->x = 0;
}

/* $0252 bank_restore, bank_scan's tail and bank_enter's exit for an absent bank: LDA $75 / STA P2, LDX $76, LDY $78,
   PLP, PLA (A as it came) */
static void bank_restore(fw *f, fw_regs *r)
{
    fw_wr_sfr8(f, SFR_P2, fw_rd8(f, FW_TMP_75));
    r->x = fw_rd8(f, FW_TMP_76);
    r->y = fw_rd8(f, FW_TMP_78);
}

void bank_enter(fw *f, fw_regs *r)
{
    fw_regs t = *r;                                                /* PHA / PHP / SEI */
    bank_select(f, &t);
    bank_probe(f, &t);
    if (fw_rd8(f, BANK_ABSENT) & 1) {                              /* BBC0 bank_absent not taken: JMP bank_restore */
        bank_restore(f, r);
        return;
    }
    uint8_t v = fw_rom8(f, BANK_HDR_VERSION + 1);                  /* LDX #$FF / TXS; LDA $FFE1 / CMP $181B */
    if (v == fw_rd8(f, BANK_VER_MINOR)) {
        v = fw_rom8(f, BANK_HDR_VERSION);                          /* LDA $FFE0 / CMP $1819 */
        if (v == fw_rd8(f, BANK_VER_MAJOR)) {
            fw_enter(f, BANK_ENTRY_WARM, v, BANK_STACK_TOP);       /* JMP $FFD4 */
            return;
        }
    }
    for (uint8_t x = BANK_ZP_CLEAR_LAST; x != 0; x--)               /* LDA #0 / LDX #$64; STA $00,X / DEX / BNE */
        fw_wr8(f, x, 0);
    fw_enter(f, BANK_ENTRY_COLD, 0, 0);                            /* JMP $FFD0 */
}

void bank_scan(fw *f, fw_regs *r)
{
    fw_regs t = *r;                                                /* PHA / PHP / SEI */
    for (t.a = BANK_COUNT; t.a != 0; t.a--) {                      /* LDA #7 ... DEC A / BNE */
        bank_select(f, &t);
        bank_probe(f, &t);
        bank_read_id(f, &t);
    }
    bank_restore(f, r);
}

void bank_select(fw *f, fw_regs *r)
{
    uint8_t v = fw_rd8(f, (uint16_t)(BANK_P2_TABLE + r->a));       /* TAX / LDA $0200,X */
    if (fw_rd8(f, BOOT_DIP) & DIP_ALT_BANKS)
        v = fw_rd8(f, (uint16_t)(BANK_P2_TABLE_ALT + r->a));       /* BBC1 boot_dip not taken: LDA $0208,X */
    uint8_t keep = fw_sfr8(f, SFR_P2) & P2_KEEP;                   /* LDA #$83 / AND P2 */
    fw_wr8(f, TN_RESUME_POS, keep);                                /* STA $77 (its scratch byte) */
    fw_wr_sfr8(f, SFR_P2, v | keep);                               /* TXA / ORA $77 / STA P2 */
    r->x = v;
}

void bank_probe(fw *f, fw_regs *r)
{
    (void)r;
    fw_wr8(f, BANK_ABSENT, fw_rd8(f, BANK_ABSENT) | 1);           /* SEB0 bank_absent */
    if (fw_rom8(f, BANK_ENTRY_COLD) == BANK_SIGNATURE)             /* LDA $FFD0 / EOR #$78 / BNE */
        fw_wr8(f, BANK_ABSENT, fw_rd8(f, BANK_ABSENT) & ~1);      /* CLB0 bank_absent */
}

void bank_read_id(fw *f, fw_regs *r)
{
    uint8_t x = r->a, sum = 0;                                     /* PHA / TAX / LDA #0 */
    while (--x != 0)                                               /* DEX / BEQ; CLC / ADC #14 / BRA */
        sum += BANK_ID_CHARS;
    fw_regs t = { 0, sum, 0, 0 };                                  /* TAX / LDY #0 */
    if (fw_rd8(f, BANK_ABSENT) & 1) {                              /* BBS0 bank_absent: 14 spaces */
        do {
            fw_wr8(f, (uint16_t)(BANK_ID_TABLE + t.x), ' ');
            t.x++;
            t.y++;
        } while (t.y < BANK_ID_CHARS);
    } else {
        do {
            t.a = fw_rom8(f, (uint16_t)(BANK_HDR_LANG_CODE + t.y));   /* LDA $FFE2,Y */
            bank_put_id_char(f, &t);
            t.y++;
        } while (t.y < BANK_LANG_CODE_CHARS);
        for (uint16_t i = 0; i < 4; i++) {                         /* LDA $9800 ... $9803 */
            t.a = fw_rom8(f, (uint16_t)(BANK_HDR_ENGINE_VER_TEXT + i));
            bank_put_id_char(f, &t);
        }
        for (uint16_t i = 0; i < 4; i++) {                         /* LDA $FFEA ... $FFED */
            t.a = fw_rom8(f, (uint16_t)(BANK_HDR_LANG_VER_TEXT + i));
            bank_put_id_char(f, &t);
        }
    }
    r->x = t.x;                                                    /* PLA: A as it came */
    r->y = t.y;
}

void bank_put_id_char(fw *f, fw_regs *r)
{
    bank_safe_char(f, r);
    fw_wr8(f, (uint16_t)(BANK_ID_TABLE + r->x), r->a);             /* STA $2026,X */
    r->x++;
}

void bank_safe_char(fw *f, fw_regs *r)
{
    (void)f;
    if (r->a < 0x20)                                               /* CMP #$20 / BCS */
        r->a = '*';
}

void bank_find_current(fw *f, fw_regs *r)
{
    (void)r;
    uint8_t bits = fw_sfr8(f, SFR_P2) & P2_BANK_BITS;              /* PHA / TXA / PHA; LDX #1 / LDA #$7C / AND P2 */
    for (uint8_t x = 1; x < BANK_COUNT + 1; x++) {                 /* INX / CPX #8 / BCC */
        uint16_t table = (fw_rd8(f, BOOT_DIP) & DIP_ALT_BANKS) ? BANK_P2_TABLE_ALT : BANK_P2_TABLE;
        if (fw_rd8(f, (uint16_t)(table + x)) == bits) {            /* CMP $0208,X or CMP $0200,X / BEQ */
            fw_wr8(f, BANK_CURRENT, x);                            /* STX bank_current */
            return;
        }
    }
}
```


with

```c

static bool run_thunk(machine *m, fw_bridge *b, thunk_regs regs) { return run_frame(m, b, regs, false); }

/* The registers in, a bank routine (bank.c, M4.6c1), the registers out: A, X and Y as the ROM leaves them. No caller
   reads a flag. */
static void bank_regs(fw *f, cpu740 *c, void (*fn)(fw *, fw_regs *))
{
    fw_regs r = { c->a, c->x, c->y, 0 };
    fn(f, &r);
    c->a = r.a;
    c->x = r.x;
    c->y = r.y;
}

/* bank_load_ramcode $A600: A, X and Y in (X and Y saved in $76 and $78). A and Y kept, X = 0 (the copy loop's INX),
   out. bank_cmd_select ($ABB2) calls bank_enter next, whose PHA and bank_select's TAX read A (the bank); boot_reset
   ($A678) calls bank_scan, which pushes A and pulls it at its exit. Neither reads X or Y: bank_restore reloads them
   from $76 and $78, and the bank entries load X first ($021F LDX #$FF, $0237 LDX #$64). Declared: A, X, Y. */
static void bank_load_ramcode_regs(fw *f, cpu740 *c) { bank_regs(f, c, bank_load_ramcode); }

/* bank_enter $0210 (RAM code): A in (the bank). For an absent bank: A kept, X = $76 and Y = $78 (bank_restore), out;
   its one caller, bank_cmd_select ($ABB5), calls bank_find_current next, which pushes A and X and pulls them back
   reading neither, then JMP cmd_run_done (PLA / TAX): none is read. A bank entry leaves the C through fw_enter (SP $FF,
   the I flag set, A and X; the run_frame's unwound branch). Declared: A, X, Y. */
static void bank_enter_regs(fw *f, cpu740 *c) { bank_regs(f, c, bank_enter); }

/* bank_scan $0241 (RAM code): A in, kept (PHA ... PLA); X = $76 and Y = $78 (bank_restore) out. Its one caller,
   boot_reset ($A67B), calls bank_find_current next (A and X kept, not read), then boot_loop_text, whose
   boot_get_clause writes X and A before reading them ($A697 LDX #1, $A699 LDA #$20) and Y at $A6EB (LDY #0).
   Declared: A, X, Y. */
static void bank_scan_regs(fw *f, cpu740 *c) { bank_regs(f, c, bank_scan); }

/* bank_select $025E (RAM code): A in (the bank), kept (PHA ... PLA); X = the table value out; Y untouched. Its callers,
   bank_enter ($0213) and bank_scan ($0246), call bank_probe next, which keeps A, X and Y and reads none (PHA, then
   LDA $FFD0). Declared: A, X, Y. */
static void bank_select_regs(fw *f, cpu740 *c) { bank_regs(f, c, bank_select); }

/* bank_probe $0277 (RAM code): A, X and Y kept (PHA ... PLA). bank_enter ($0216) tests bank_absent next and then
   reloads A, X and Y (bank_restore, or LDX #$FF and LDA $FFE1); bank_scan ($0249) calls bank_read_id, which reads A
   (the bank: PHA / TAX). Declared: A, X, Y. */
static void bank_probe_regs(fw *f, cpu740 *c) { bank_regs(f, c, bank_probe); }

/* bank_read_id $0285 (RAM code): A in (the bank), kept; X after the last ID byte and Y = 6 (present) or 14 (absent)
   out. Its one caller, bank_scan ($024C), reads A (DEC A / BNE) and then calls bank_select (TAX first) or falls into
   bank_restore (LDX, LDY): X and Y are not read. Declared: A, X, Y. */
static void bank_read_id_regs(fw *f, cpu740 *c) { bank_regs(f, c, bank_read_id); }

/* bank_put_id_char $02E1 (RAM code): A and X in; A through bank_safe_char, X + 1, Y untouched, out. Its caller,
   bank_read_id ($029A ... $02CF), reads Y (INY / CPY #6) and X (the next STA $2026,X, or its own exit) and loads A
   next. Declared: A, X, Y. */
static void bank_put_id_char_regs(fw *f, cpu740 *c) { bank_regs(f, c, bank_put_id_char); }

/* bank_safe_char $02E9 (RAM code): A in; A ('*' below $20) out; X and Y untouched. Its caller, bank_put_id_char
   ($02E1), stores A at $2026,X. Declared: A, X, Y. */
```

```c

#define FW_RAM_SIZE  0x4000
#define FW_TMP_78    0x0078   /* zero-page scratch, unnamed in the annotations: a saved Y, byte counters;
                                 par_scale_duration's saved Y ($C2A0; not written for a duration of 0) */
#define FW_TMP_75    0x0075   /* zero-page scratch, unnamed in the annotations: tn_convert_clause's saved X;
                                 isr_set_volume's inverted level << 3 ($ACEF);
                                 frm_clamp_mark_space's 2 x b10 ($B936), frm_raise_formants' (b10 - 32) / 4 ($B969)
                                 and frm_transition_flags' count of the rising amplitudes ($BAA7-$BAB7) */
```

### Task 5: The dispatcher in C: `cmd_run_next` $A893 (with its hook) and `bank_cmd_select` $ABAF (in the C handler table); the C `cmd_run_due` calls the C `cmd_run_next`, which hands only a NUL-bug address to the ROM through an unhooked call; the tests that recorded the ROM `cmd_run_next` updated; the Review Focus tests; the branch probe; the CLI check (spec §2.1–§2.5, §4.1, §4.3–§4.5, §4.7, §4.8 item 5)

**ROM facts checked / what was verified while proving the plan** (`rom_A893_cmd_run_next.txt`, `rom_A880_cmd_run_due.txt`, `rom_ABAF_bank_cmd_select.txt`, `rom_ABBE_cmd_list_languages.txt`, `rom_B1E6_tn_convert_clause.txt`; `cmd_match.c`, `cmd_data.c`; the ROM image for the operand scans; experiments with both engines on raw serial input, a throwaway program not part of the plan):
- **`cmd_run_next`** $A893–$A8BF: `TXA / PHA`; `cmd_vector` from `cmd_handlers[cmd_next × 2]` (an 8-bit index); `INC cmd_next`; `cmd_next_pos` = `cmd_positions[cmd_next]`; X = `cmd_arg_index[cmd_next − 1]`, A = `boot_clause[X]`; `JMP ($1489)`; every handler ends at `cmd_run_done` $A8BD: `PLA / TAX / RTS`, so A = X = the X it pushed. It never touches Y, so a handler starts with Y = cmd_next before the `INC` (`cmd_handler_y`). Its one caller is `cmd_run_due` $A88D (operand scan), which branches back to `LDY cmd_next / CPY cmd_count / CPX cmd_next_pos` (X read, Y written) and returns to `tn_convert_clause` ($B20F `CPX tn_pos`, then `LDA $1A00,X`): the row declares A and X.
- **`bank_cmd_select`** $ABAF–$ABBD: `JSR cmd_hex_value` (A = the bank; X and Y kept), `JSR bank_load_ramcode` (it saves X = the argument index and Y = cmd_next − 1 in $76 and $78), `JSR bank_enter`, `JSR bank_find_current`, `JMP cmd_run_done`. No `JSR` or `JMP` names it; the pattern record `"=#"` holds its address (`cmd_pat_bank_fn` $9934), so it is reached only through `cmd_run_next`'s `JMP ($1489)` and joins the C handler table, unhooked like the M4.2 handlers. Its pattern takes a digit only (`#`: '0'–'9' after the case map, `cmd_match.c`), so its argument is 0–9: 0 reads `bank_p2_table[0]` and 8–9 `bank_p2_table_alt[0–1]`, all $70: `@=0`, `@=8` and `@=9` enter the main bank warm; `@=F` and `@=G` match no pattern and stay text.
- **The NUL bug** (M4.2 spec 2.6): `" @A"` then a zero queues $D300 (from inside the pattern `"*?"`), `" @M5"` then a zero queues $002A. The C `cmd_run_next` leaves RAM untouched for every address with no C handler and calls the ROM `cmd_run_next` through `fw_rom_call_unhooked` with A = X = the position and Y = cmd_next; its own hook does not fire on that entry (`test_golden_hybrid`'s bridge test (2)). On raw serial input after boot (Step 4, `test_review_focus`): `"@M5" NUL CR` ends with the same CPU fault on both engines (a `BRK`, opcode $00, at $2300) and no frame; `"@A" NUL CR` ends with no frame on both, but the ROM engine faults on an illegal opcode $54 at $8F02 while the hybrid faults at $A893 (`MFAULT_FIRMWARE`): the code at $D300 returns through the stack, and the C dispatcher's frames (`tn_convert_clause`'s `JSR cmd_run_due`) are not on the machine stack, so its `RTS`s go elsewhere and the unhooked ROM call never comes back within its budget. The hybrid at 7435fa5 (the M4.2 fallback through `fw_rom_call`) ends the same way on the same input (the throwaway program, run on both trees), so this is the divergence the spec keeps (§2.5: the C cannot follow; M5 decides), and Review Focus 5 is met only as far as `test_review_focus` pins it.
- **`@L`** (`cmd_list_languages` $ABBE) reads `bank_id_table` only: "L", 7 and 14 as hex digits low first ("70E0"), the 98 ID bytes, CR: 104 bytes queued in the output ring. The machine never raises the host line, so a reply is never transmitted; `test_review_focus` compares the queued bytes (read pointer to write pointer). After a warm `@=1` the rest of its clause is abandoned with the C chain, so `"@=1 @L"` queues nothing on either engine.
- **The tests that recorded the ROM `cmd_run_next`**: `test_fw_cmd.c` (its queue tests used `bank_cmd_select` as the ROM handler), `test_fw_tn.c` (its queued commands), `test_golden_hybrid.c`'s bridge tests (2) and (4) (a hook at $A893): they now queue the NUL-bug addresses $D300 and $002A and the mocks answer `rom_call_unhooked` (in `test_fw_cmd.c` a hooked `rom_call` is counted and must not happen); `test_run_due_all_rows` runs `bank_cmd_select` as C (`"@A5"`: bank 5, an empty socket). The bridge test (5) has the C enter a bank on a fresh machine: version copies 0 (cold, zero page cleared) and 2.01 (warm).
- **`diff_lts`** with `cmd_run_next` in `k_every_call` and the Review Focus texts 1, 2 and 4 in `k_extra` (Step 4): `cmd_run_next 165 calls checked` and the bank routines' counts of Step 4, `24 unwound calls compared` (the extra texts' `@=0`, `@=8`, `@=9`, `@=1` under `tn_convert_clause`, `cmd_run_due`, `cmd_run_next` and `bank_enter`), 0 mismatches; 179 s in the proof run.

```c
/* ---- the dispatcher (M4.6c1 Task 5) ---- */

/* Queues command n at position pos with handler h and its argument at boot_clause[arg] = c; cmd_count = n + 1,
   cmd_next_pos = cmd_positions[0]. */
static void queue(uint8_t n, uint8_t pos, uint16_t h, uint8_t arg, char c)
{
    g_ram[CMD_POSITIONS + n] = pos;
    g_ram[CMD_HANDLERS + 2 * n] = (uint8_t)h;
    g_ram[CMD_HANDLERS + 2 * n + 1] = (uint8_t)(h >> 8);
    g_ram[CMD_ARG_INDEX + n] = arg;
    g_ram[BOOT_CLAUSE + arg] = (uint8_t)c;
    g_ram[CMD_COUNT] = (uint8_t)(n + 1);
    g_ram[CMD_NEXT_POS] = g_ram[CMD_POSITIONS];
}

/* The cmd_run_next row: A and X declared (cmd_run_done), a main-ROM hook. */
static void test_run_next_row(void)
{
    const fw_replacement *r = fw_replacement_find("cmd_run_next");
    CHECK(r != NULL);
    if (!r)
        return;
    CHECK_EQ(r->addr, ROM_CMD_RUN_NEXT);
    CHECK_EQ(r->outputs, FW_OUT_A | FW_OUT_X);
    CHECK(!r->waits && !r->isr && !r->ramcode);
}

/* cmd_run_next with an address no C handler has (the scan's NUL bug, $D300 after "@A" and a zero; M4.6c1 spec 2.5 and
   4.3): RAM untouched (cmd_vector, cmd_next and cmd_next_pos are the ROM's to write), one unhooked ROM call of $A893
   with X = the position, Y = cmd_next and A = X; a failed call is a fault; an unwound one stops cmd_run_due. */
static void test_run_next_unknown(void)
{
    static const event want[] = { { EV_CALL, ROM_CMD_RUN_NEXT, 4, 4, 1 } };
    setup();
    queue(1, 4, 0xD300, 3, 'x');
    g_ram[CMD_NEXT] = 1;
    static uint8_t before[0x10000];
    memcpy(before, g_ram, sizeof before);
    CHECK(cmd_run_next(&g_f, 4));
    check_log(want, 1);
    CHECK(memcmp(before, g_ram, sizeof before) == 0);
    CHECK(!g_f.fault);

    setup();
    queue(0, 0, 0x002A, 3, 'x');
    g_call_result = FW_CALL_FAILED;
    CHECK(!cmd_run_next(&g_f, 0));
    CHECK(g_f.fault);
    CHECK_EQ(g_f.fault_addr, ROM_CMD_RUN_NEXT);

    setup();
    queue(0, 0, 0xD300, 3, 'x');
    queue(1, 0, 0xD300, 3, 'x');
    g_call_result = FW_CALL_UNWOUND;
    cmd_run_due(&g_f, 0);
    CHECK_EQ(g_nlog, 1);                          /* the second is not run */
    CHECK(g_f.unwound);
    CHECK(!g_f.fault);
}

/* bank_cmd_select through cmd_run_next (the C dispatch of $ABAF): "@=5" (an empty socket) loads the RAM copy, selects
   and probes bank 5, restores P2 and finds the current bank (P2 read once more), and the next queued command runs;
   "@=1" with the versions equal enters the main bank warm ($FFD4, A = 2, X = $FF) and the next command does not
   run. bank_load_ramcode saved Y = cmd_next before the INC (cmd_handler_y) and X = the argument index. */
static void test_cmd_select(void)
{
    static const event absent[] = {
        { EV_READ, SFR_P2, P2_MAIN_BANK, 0, 0 },                                    /* bank_load_ramcode */
        { EV_READ, SFR_P2, P2_MAIN_BANK, 0, 0 }, { EV_WRITE, SFR_P2, P2_BANK_5, 0, 0 },   /* bank_select */
        { EV_BANK, BANK_ENTRY_COLD, 0xFF, 0, 0 },                                   /* bank_probe */
        { EV_WRITE, SFR_P2, P2_MAIN_BANK, 0, 0 },                                   /* bank_restore */
        { EV_READ, SFR_P2, P2_MAIN_BANK, 0, 0 },                                    /* bank_find_current */
    };
    static const event warm[] = {
        { EV_READ, SFR_P2, P2_MAIN_BANK, 0, 0 },
        { EV_READ, SFR_P2, P2_MAIN_BANK, 0, 0 }, { EV_WRITE, SFR_P2, P2_MAIN_BANK, 0, 0 },
        { EV_ENTER, BANK_ENTRY_WARM, 2, 0xFF, 0 },
    };
    setup_banks();
    memset(g_ram + BANK_RAMCODE, 0, BANK_RAMCODE_LAST - BANK_RAMCODE + 1);   /* speech overwrote the copy */
    g_ram[BANK_CURRENT] = 7;
    queue(0, 2, ROM_BANK_CMD_SELECT, 6, '5');
    queue(1, 2, ROM_CMD_SET_SPELL, 9, '1');
    cmd_run_due(&g_f, 2);
    check_log(absent, 6);
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[FW_TMP_78], 0);                /* Y = cmd_next before the INC */
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[FW_TMP_76], 6);                /* X = the argument index */
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[BANK_P2_TABLE + 5], P2_BANK_5);   /* reloaded before bank_select read it (Review Focus 4) */
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[BANK_CURRENT], 1);
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[CMD_SPELL], 1);                /* the next command ran */
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[CMD_NEXT], 2);
    CHECK(!g_f.unwound);
    CHECK(!g_f.fault);

    setup_banks();
    queue(0, 2, ROM_BANK_CMD_SELECT, 6, '1');
    queue(1, 2, ROM_CMD_SET_SPELL, 9, '1');
    cmd_run_due(&g_f, 2);
    check_log(warm, 4);
    CHECK(g_f.unwound);
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[CMD_SPELL], 0);                /* abandoned with the C */
    CHECK_EQ(g_ram[CMD_NEXT], 1);
    CHECK(!g_f.fault);
}

void suite_fw_bank(void)
{
    CHECK(fw_image_get() != NULL);
```

```c
   P0 ($F6), recorded as a call of ROM_ISR_PAR_READY without registers (the C passes them to nothing). P2 ($F0) is the
   bank_cmd_select's (g_p2). Any other SFR access fails the test. */
static uint8_t mock_read_sfr(void *ctx, uint8_t addr)
{
    (void)ctx;
    if (addr == 0xF0)
        return g_p2;
    CHECK_EQ(addr, 0xF6);
    return g_p0;
}
```

```c
#define BOOT_RESET_ADDR 0xA61C   /* any ROM address: where reset_stack_hook "jumps" */

/* One command queued, due at position 0, whose handler stays in ROM (bank_cmd_select): the C cmd_run_due with X = 0
   hands it to the ROM cmd_run_next, its one ROM call. */
static void queue_rom_command(machine *m)
{
    m->ram[CMD_COUNT] = 1;
    m->ram[CMD_NEXT] = 0;
    m->ram[CMD_NEXT_POS] = 0;
    m->ram[CMD_HANDLERS] = ROM_BANK_CMD_SELECT & 0xFF;
    m->ram[CMD_HANDLERS + 1] = ROM_BANK_CMD_SELECT >> 8;
    m->cpu.x = 0;
}
```

with

```c
}

#define BOOT_RESET_ADDR 0xA61C   /* any ROM address: where reset_stack_hook "jumps" */
#define NUL_BUG_ADDR    0xD300   /* a handler address of the scan's NUL bug ("@A" then a zero, M4.6c1 spec 2.5) */

/* One command queued, due at position 0, with the handler handler and its argument at boot_clause[1] = arg: the C
   cmd_run_due with X = 0 runs it through the C cmd_run_next (an unknown address: the ROM cmd_run_next, unhooked). */
static void queue_command(machine *m, uint16_t handler, char arg)
{
    m->ram[CMD_COUNT] = 1;
    m->ram[CMD_NEXT] = 0;
    m->ram[CMD_NEXT_POS] = 0;
    m->ram[CMD_HANDLERS] = (uint8_t)handler;
    m->ram[CMD_HANDLERS + 1] = (uint8_t)(handler >> 8);
    m->ram[CMD_ARG_INDEX] = 1;
    m->ram[BOOT_CLAUSE + 1] = (uint8_t)arg;
    m->cpu.x = 0;
}

static unsigned g_run_next_hooks;

/* An observer at cmd_run_next's own address: counts and lets the ROM run (the unhooked call must skip it). */
static bool count_hook(machine *m, void *user)
{
    (void)m;
    (void)user;
    g_run_next_hooks++;
    return false;
}

static bool reset_stack_hook(machine *m, void *user)
{
```

```c
    /* (5) the C enters a bank (M4.6c1 spec 3.2, 3.4): @=1 dispatched by the C cmd_run_due on a fresh machine (P2's
       pins $70: the main bank). Its version copies are 0, not the header's 2.01: the cold entry, zero page $01-$64
       cleared; with them set, the warm entry. The machine goes on at the entry with SP $FF, I set, A and X as
       bank_enter leaves them; no fault; the chain unwound once. */

/* ---- the M4.6c1 plan's Review Focus: the rom and hybrid engines on the same input ---- */

typedef struct {
    machine_status st;
    machine_fault fault;
    uint16_t fault_addr;     /* MFAULT_FIRMWARE */
    uint8_t cpu_fault, opcode;
    uint16_t cpu_fault_pc;   /* MFAULT_CPU */
    char *frames;            /* frame_list_hex */
    uint8_t reply[256];      /* the output ring from its read pointer to its write pointer: the queued reply */
    unsigned reply_len;
    uint8_t tx_pending, bank_current, bank_ids[98];
    unsigned unwinds;
} outcome;

static fw_bridge g_raw_bridge;
static frame_list g_raw_frames;

static void install_all(machine *m, void *user)
{
    (void)user;
    CHECK(fw_bridge_init(&g_raw_bridge, m) && fw_install(m, &g_raw_bridge, NULL));
}

static void collect(void *user, const uint8_t f[APOLLO_FRAME_BYTES])
{
    frame_list *fl = user;
    if (fl->count == fl->cap) {
        fl->cap = fl->cap ? fl->cap * 2 : 256;
        fl->frames = realloc(fl->frames, fl->cap * sizeof fl->frames[0]);
        if (!fl->frames)
            abort();
    }
    memcpy(fl->frames[fl->count++], f, APOLLO_FRAME_BYTES);
}

/* One engine (hybrid: every replacement) booted to idle, then the n raw bytes of in fed to the serial input (a NUL
   included: no CLI text can hold one) and run to idle, a fault or the speaking budget. */
static void run_raw(const uint8_t *rom, size_t len, bool hybrid, const char *in, size_t n, outcome *o)
{
    frame_list boot = { 0 };
    memset(o, 0, sizeof *o);
    memset(&g_raw_bridge, 0, sizeof g_raw_bridge);
    o->st = rom_engine_run(&g_m, rom, len, NULL, hybrid ? install_all : NULL, NULL, "", false, &boot, NULL);
    frame_list_free(&boot);
    CHECK_EQ(o->st, MACHINE_IDLE);
    machine_set_outputs(&g_m, collect, &g_raw_frames);
    CHECK(machine_feed_serial(&g_m, (const uint8_t *)in, n));
    o->st = machine_run(&g_m, ROM_ENGINE_SPEAK_CYCLES);
    machine_set_outputs(&g_m, NULL, NULL);
    machine_clear_hooks(&g_m);
    o->fault = g_m.fault;
    o->fault_addr = g_m.fault_addr;
    o->cpu_fault = (uint8_t)g_m.cpu.fault;
    o->opcode = g_m.cpu.fault_opcode;
    o->cpu_fault_pc = g_m.cpu.fault_pc;
    o->frames = frame_list_hex(&g_raw_frames);
    frame_list_free(&g_raw_frames);
    uint16_t rd = (uint16_t)(g_m.ram[SER_TX_READ] | g_m.ram[SER_TX_READ + 1] << 8);
    uint16_t wr = (uint16_t)(g_m.ram[SER_TX_WRITE] | g_m.ram[SER_TX_WRITE + 1] << 8);
    for (uint16_t a = rd; a != wr && o->reply_len < sizeof o->reply; a = (uint16_t)(a == 0x39FF ? 0x3900 : a + 1))
        o->reply[o->reply_len++] = g_m.ram[a];
    o->tx_pending = g_m.ram[SER_TX_PENDING];
    o->bank_current = g_m.ram[BANK_CURRENT];
    memcpy(o->bank_ids, g_m.ram + BANK_ID_TABLE, sizeof o->bank_ids);
    o->unwinds = (unsigned)g_raw_bridge.unwinds;
}

/* Both engines on in: the same status, fault, frames, queued reply and bank state. Returns the hybrid's unwinds. */
static unsigned check_same(const uint8_t *rom, size_t len, const char *name, const char *in, size_t n, outcome *a)
{
    outcome b;
    run_raw(rom, len, false, in, n, a);
    run_raw(rom, len, true, in, n, &b);
    bool same = a->st == b.st && a->fault == b.fault && a->fault_addr == b.fault_addr &&
                a->cpu_fault == b.cpu_fault && a->opcode == b.opcode && a->cpu_fault_pc == b.cpu_fault_pc &&
                !strcmp(a->frames, b.frames) && a->reply_len == b.reply_len &&
                !memcmp(a->reply, b.reply, a->reply_len) && a->tx_pending == b.tx_pending &&
                a->bank_current == b.bank_current && !memcmp(a->bank_ids, b.bank_ids, sizeof a->bank_ids);
    if (!same) {
        fprintf(stderr, "  review %s: rom status %d fault %d, hybrid status %d fault %d (or frames, reply, banks)\n",
                name, (int)a->st, (int)a->fault, (int)b.st, (int)b.fault);
        g_test_failures++;
    }
    free(b.frames);
    return b.unwinds;
}

#define TEXT(s) s "\r", sizeof s   /* the text and its CR: the bytes the CLI feeds */

/* The Review Focus inputs (M4.6c1 plan): 1. @= with 0, 8, 9 (bank_select indexes $0200,X past the seven real
   entries: $0200 and $0208-$0209 hold the main bank, so each enters warm) and with F and G (no digit: "=#" does not
   match); 2. several @= and text after a warm re-entry; 3. @L after the banks change (the reply queued for the host:
   the machine never raises the host line, so it stays in the output ring); 4. speech, then @= (the segments
   overwrite the RAM copy first); 5. a NUL after a command prefix, raw serial input: no frames on either engine.
   "@M5" NUL: the same CPU fault on both (a BRK at $2300, the zero page address the scan queued). "@A" NUL: the ROM
   cmd_run_next jumps to $D300 inside pros_phrase_gen, whose code returns through the stack: the ROM engine's into
   $8F02 (an illegal opcode $54), the hybrid's elsewhere, since the C dispatcher's frames are not on the machine
   stack; the hybrid's unhooked ROM call then never returns, a firmware fault at $A893 (the C cannot follow the
   jump; M4.6c1 spec 2.5, unchanged from M4.2). */
static void test_review_focus(const uint8_t *rom, size_t len)
{
    outcome a;
    CHECK_EQ(check_same(rom, len, "1", TEXT("@=0 Zero. @=8 Eight. @=9 Nine. @=F Eff. @=G Gee."), &a), 3);
    CHECK_EQ(a.st, MACHINE_IDLE);
    free(a.frames);
    CHECK_EQ(check_same(rom, len, "2", TEXT("@=1 One. @=5 Two. @=1 Three."), &a), 2);
    CHECK_EQ(a.st, MACHINE_IDLE);
    CHECK(strlen(a.frames) > 0);
    free(a.frames);
    static const char *const k_l[] = { "@=5 @L\r", "@=1 @L\r", "@=1 One. @L @=5 Two. @L\r" };
    static const unsigned k_reply[] = { 104, 0, 208 };   /* "L70E0", 7 x 14 ID characters, CR; none: the warm
                                                            re-entry abandons the rest of its clause; two */
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        check_same(rom, len, "3", k_l[i], strlen(k_l[i]), &a);
        CHECK_EQ(a.reply_len, k_reply[i]);
        CHECK(a.reply_len == 0 || !memcmp(a.reply, "L70E000044002010404", 19));
        free(a.frames);
    }
    CHECK_EQ(check_same(rom, len, "4", TEXT("This sentence is spoken first, and its segments overwrite the bank code. "
                                            "@=5 Then more speech follows the bank command."), &a), 0);
    CHECK_EQ(a.st, MACHINE_IDLE);
    free(a.frames);

    check_same(rom, len, "5", "@M5\0\r", 5, &a);
    CHECK_EQ(a.st, MACHINE_FAULT);
    CHECK_EQ(a.fault, MFAULT_CPU);
    CHECK_EQ(a.opcode, 0x00);
    CHECK_EQ(a.cpu_fault_pc, 0x2300);
    CHECK_STR(a.frames, "");
    free(a.frames);
    outcome b;
    run_raw(rom, len, false, "@A\0\r", 4, &a);
    run_raw(rom, len, true, "@A\0\r", 4, &b);
    CHECK_EQ(a.st, MACHINE_FAULT);
    CHECK_EQ(a.fault, MFAULT_CPU);
    CHECK_EQ(a.opcode, 0x54);
    CHECK_EQ(a.cpu_fault_pc, 0x8F02);
    CHECK_EQ(b.st, MACHINE_FAULT);
    CHECK_EQ(b.fault, MFAULT_FIRMWARE);
    CHECK_EQ(b.fault_addr, ROM_CMD_RUN_NEXT);
    CHECK_STR(a.frames, "");
    CHECK_STR(b.frames, "");
    free(a.frames);
    free(b.frames);
}

void suite_golden_hybrid(void)
```

```c
   runs as C. Any other command (bank_cmd_select, an unknown address) runs through the ROM cmd_run_next, which
```

```c
/* $A880: x = a cmd_text position. Runs every queued command due there (while cmd_next < cmd_count and
   cmd_next_pos = x), in queue order, through cmd_run_next; it stops when a command abandoned the C (a bank entry).
   X is kept (cmd_run_done); the ROM leaves Y = cmd_next. */

   x and Y = cmd_next, which does its own bookkeeping and jumps where it jumps. X (= x) is kept. False when the C was
   abandoned (a failed or unwound ROM call, a bank entry); that only returns here without a trap. */
```

```c
void cmd_run_due(fw *f, uint8_t x)
{
    while (fw_rd8(f, CMD_NEXT) < fw_rd8(f, CMD_COUNT) && x == fw_rd8(f, CMD_NEXT_POS)) {
        if (!run_next(f, x))
            return;
```

```c
{
```

```c
    bank_load_ramcode(f, &r);                                      /* JSR bank_load_ramcode */

    bank_find_current(f, &r);                                      /* JSR bank_find_current; JMP cmd_run_done */
```

```c
/* isr_set_volume $ACE8: A in (the volume). A = the value written to P3 ($ACF7 STA P3, after AND P3 / ORA $75), X and
   Y untouched, out. boot_reset ($A642) calls boot_read_dip next, which loads A first ($ADE4 LDA P4) and reads no X

```

with

```c
    c->y = fw_rd8(f, CMD_NEXT);

/* cmd_run_next $A893: X in (the position). A = X = the X it pushed (cmd_run_done $A8BD: PLA / TAX) out; Y is the
   handler's (no C handler sets it; the NUL-bug fallback leaves the ROM's). Its one ROM caller, cmd_run_due ($A88D,
   ROM where it is not replaced), branches back to $A880 (LDY cmd_next, then CPX cmd_next_pos: X read, Y written) and
   returns to tn_convert_clause, which loads A before reading it ($B20F CPX tn_pos, LDA $1A00,X). No caller reads a
   flag. A bank entry (bank_cmd_select) leaves the C through fw_enter. Declared: A, X. */
static void cmd_run_next_regs(fw *f, cpu740 *c)
{
    uint8_t x = c->x;
    cmd_run_next(f, x);
    c->a = x;
    c->x = x;
}
```

Then spec §4.5 on every corpus text and the Review Focus texts (each corpus text read from its file, the WAV files in a scratch folder outside the repo, always with `-o`):

```bash
SP="${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/m46c1_cli"
mkdir -p "$SP"
for f in tests/corpus/*.txt; do
  t=$(basename "$f" .txt)
  ./build/apollo2.exe --engine rom --text-file "$f" -o "$SP/${t}_rom.wav" || echo "$t rom failed"
  ./build/apollo2.exe --engine hybrid --text-file "$f" -o "$SP/${t}_hybrid.wav" || echo "$t hybrid failed"
  cmp -s "$SP/${t}_rom.wav" "$SP/${t}_hybrid.wav" && echo "$t same" || echo "$t DIFFERS"
done | grep -vc " same$"
i=0
while IFS= read -r t; do
  i=$((i+1))
  ./build/apollo2.exe --engine rom -o "$SP/rf${i}_rom.wav" "$t" || echo "rf$i rom failed"
  ./build/apollo2.exe --engine hybrid -o "$SP/rf${i}_hybrid.wav" "$t" || echo "rf$i hybrid failed"
  cmp -s "$SP/rf${i}_rom.wav" "$SP/rf${i}_hybrid.wav" && echo "rf$i same" || echo "rf$i DIFFERS"
done <<'EOF'
@=0 Zero. @=8 Eight. @=9 Nine. @=F Eff. @=G Gee.
@=1 One. @=5 Two. @=1 Three.
@=5 @L
@=1 @L
This sentence is spoken first, and its segments overwrite the bank code. @=5 Then more speech follows the bank command.
EOF
rm -rf "$SP"
```

Expected: `0` (all 47 texts `same`; no `failed` or `DIFFERS` line), then `rf1 same` … `rf5 same`. The proof run took 13 s.

Last, spec §4.7 criterion 1: `grep -n "fw_rom_call\|fw_enter" src/firmware/bank.c src/firmware/cmd_run.c` prints exactly `bank.c`'s two `fw_enter` calls (`BANK_ENTRY_WARM`, `BANK_ENTRY_COLD`) and `cmd_run.c`'s `return fw_rom_call_unhooked(f, ROM_CMD_RUN_NEXT, &r, FW_ROM_CALL_BUDGET);`, and `grep -rn "fw_rom_call(" src/firmware` prints, besides its declaration (`fw.h`) and definition (`fw.c`), only `cmd_play_event.c`'s call of an unknown event handler (M4.4b, unchanged): no other ROM call to firmware code.
